## `AFG-1391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Bursting-Strength Tester
- **Scenario:** Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Knitted Fabric Loop Length & Dimensional Shrinkage QC
- **Intro:** Bursting-Strength Tester — Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Knitted Fabric Loop Length & Dimensional Shrinkage QC — Receive and Verify Raw Material Batch Documentation: Batch ID must match the purchase order exactly. CoA must indicate a bursting strength of ≥ 350 kPa for the specified fabric we…
- **Key tolerance:** Batch ID must match the purchase order exactly. CoA must indicate a bursting strength of ≥ 350 kPa for the specified fabric weight (e.g., 180 g/m² ± 5%).
- **Standards:** `ISO 13938-1:2019`, `ISO 6330:2021`, `ISO 3759:2011`, `ASTM D3884-09 Standard Guide for Abrasion Testing of Textile Fab`

- **Role:** Needle Safety Officer
